126:def damaged_components_by_layer(
127:    ship: 'ShipInstance',
128:) -> Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, int]]]:
129:    """Return damaged component instances grouped by layer.
130:
131:    PROJ-425 Phase 2: extracted from ``ShipInstance.get_damaged_components_by_layer``.
132:
133:    Walks the design's layers to map each ``component_id`` to its layer,
134:    then iterates per-instance ``ship.components`` picking out the ones
135:    whose ``current_hp`` is below ``max_hp``.
136:
137:    Returns a dict mapping layer name to a list of
138:    ``(component_state_key, current_hp)`` tuples for each damaged instance.
139:    """
140:    damaged_states = [
141:        (key, cs) for key, cs in ship.components.items() if cs.is_damaged
142:    ]
143:    if not damaged_states:
144:        return {}
145:
146:    comp_id_to_layer: Dict[str, str] = {}
147:    for layer_name, components in ship.design_data.get('layers', {}).items():
148:        for comp_entry in components:
149:            comp_id = (
150:                comp_entry.get('id') if isinstance(comp_entry, dict)
151:                else comp_entry
152:            )
153:            if comp_id:
154:                comp_id_to_layer[comp_id] = layer_name
155:
156:    result: Dict[str, List[Tuple[str, int]]] = {}
157:    for key, cs in damaged_states:
158:        layer_name = comp_id_to_layer.get(cs.component_id, 'UNKNOWN')
159:        result.setdefault(layer_name, []).append((key, int(cs.current_hp)))
160:
161:    return result

61:def extract_abilities_from_component(comp: Any, registries=None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
62:    """Extract the abilities dict from a design_data component entry.
63:
64:    Handles both inline abilities and registry lookup by component ID.
65:    This is the single source of truth for extracting abilities from
66:    component entries in design_data dicts.
67:
68:    Args:
69:        comp: Component entry from design_data layers (dict or str).
70:        registries: Optional - either GameRegistries (with .components attr)
71:                    or a plain dict acting as component registry.
72:
73:    Returns:
74:        Dict of abilities keyed by ability name (empty dict if none found).
75:    """
76:    if isinstance(comp, dict):
77:        abilities = comp.get('abilities', {})
78:        if abilities:
79:            return abilities
80:        comp_id = comp.get('id')
81:        if comp_id and registries is not None:
82:            comp_reg = _get_component_registry(registries)
83:            comp_def = comp_reg.get(comp_id) if comp_reg else None
84:            if comp_def is not None:
85:                return get_component_abilities(comp_def)
86:    elif isinstance(comp, str) and registries is not None:
87:        comp_reg = _get_component_registry(registries)
88:        comp_def = comp_reg.get(comp) if comp_reg else None
89:        if comp_def is not None:
90:            return get_component_abilities(comp_def)
91:    return {}

144:def iterate_design_components(
145:    design_data: Dict[str, Any],
146:    component_registry: Dict[str, Any]
147:) -> Iterator[Tuple[Dict[str, Any], Any, Dict[str, Any]]]:
148:    """Iterate through all components in a design, yielding component details.
149:
150:    This is the canonical way to iterate over design components with their
151:    definitions and abilities. Handles various layer formats.
152:
153:    Supports both patterns:
154:    1. Component ID lookup: comp_entry has 'id' field, abilities from registry
155:    2. Inline abilities: comp_entry has 'abilities' field directly (test mocks)
156:
157:    Args:
158:        design_data: Ship/facility design data dict with 'layers' key
159:        component_registry: Component registry for looking up definitions
160:
161:    Yields:
162:        Tuple of (comp_entry, comp_def, abilities):
163:        - comp_entry: The raw component entry dict from design_data
164:        - comp_def: The component definition from registry (may be None)
165:        - abilities: Dict of abilities from component definition OR inline
166:    """
167:    layers = design_data.get('layers', {})
168:
169:    for layer_components in layers.values():
170:        # Skip layers that aren't lists (handles both formats)
171:        if not isinstance(layer_components, list):
172:            continue
173:
174:        for comp_entry in layer_components:
175:            # Extract component ID from entry
176:            if isinstance(comp_entry, dict):
177:                comp_id = comp_entry.get('id', '')
178:            else:
179:                # Handle string-only entries if they exist
180:                comp_id = str(comp_entry) if comp_entry else ''
181:
182:            # Look up component definition from registry
183:            comp_def = component_registry.get(comp_id) if comp_id else None
184:
185:            # Get abilities from registry definition first
186:            abilities = get_component_abilities(comp_def)
187:
188:            # Fallback: check for inline abilities in comp_entry (test mocks)
189:            if not abilities and isinstance(comp_entry, dict):
190:                abilities = comp_entry.get('abilities', {})
191:
192:            yield comp_entry, comp_def, abilities

195:def ship_has_ability(
196:    ship: 'ShipInstance',
197:    ability_name: str,
198:    component_registry: Dict[str, Any]
199:) -> bool:
200:    """Check if a ship has a component with a specific ability.
201:
202:    Args:
203:        ship: Ship object with design_data attribute
204:        ability_name: Name of ability to check for (e.g., "ColonyPod", "DestroyPlanet")
205:        component_registry: Component registry for ability lookup

289:def get_ability_list(

293:    """Get ability data as a list (handles both single and multiple abilities).
294:
295:    Normalizes ability data formats: single dicts become one-element lists,
296:    scalar values become [{'value': val}], lists pass through unchanged.
297:
298:    Args:
299:        abilities: Dict of all abilities on a component
300:        ability_name: Name of the ability to extract

315:def iter_facility_ability_entries(
316:    facility: Any,
317:    ability_name: str,
318:    registries: Any = None,
319:) -> Iterator[Tuple[Any, Dict[str, Any]]]:
320:    """Iterate (component, ability_entry) pairs for one ability on a facility.
321:
322:    PROJ-375 Task 2.1: consolidates the iterate->extract->read-field pattern that
323:    was hand-rolled in 8+ engines. Walks the facility's design_data layers via
324:    `iter_components`, resolves each component's abilities (inline OR via
325:    registry lookup), and yields one normalized dict entry per ability instance.
326:
327:    Normalization rules for the ability data shape:
328:    - dict        -> yielded once as-is.
329:    - list[dict]  -> yielded once per element.
330:    - list of non-dicts (rare) -> each element wrapped as `{"value": x}`.
331:    - scalar      -> wrapped as `{"value": x}` and yielded once.
332:    - missing / None -> yields nothing for that component.
333:
334:    The component is yielded alongside the entry so callers that need
335:    component-level metadata (e.g. `resolve_size_multiplier(comp)`) get it
336:    without a second lookup.
337:
338:    Args:
339:        facility: Object exposing a `design_data` dict (PlanetaryFacility,
340:            ShipDesign, or any test mock with `design_data`).
341:        ability_name: Ability key to extract (e.g. "WaterModifier",
342:            "ResourceHarvester", "PlanetaryShield").
343:        registries: Optional `GameRegistries` (with `.components`) or a
344:            plain dict acting as the component registry â€” passed through
345:            to `extract_abilities_from_component` for ID-based lookups.
346:
347:    Yields:
348:        Tuples of `(component_entry, ability_entry_dict)`.
349:    """
350:    # Local import keeps this module free of strategy-engine imports.
351:    from game.core.patterns.layer_iterator import iter_components
352:
353:    for comp in iter_components(facility.design_data):
354:        abilities = extract_abilities_from_component(comp, registries)
355:        data = abilities.get(ability_name)
356:        if data is None:
357:            continue
358:        if isinstance(data, dict):
359:            yield comp, data
360:        elif isinstance(data, list):
361:            for entry in data:
362:                if isinstance(entry, dict):
363:                    yield comp, entry
364:                else:
365:                    yield comp, {"value": entry}
366:        else:
367:            yield comp, {"value": data}
368:
369:
370:def has_warp_capability(ship: Any) -> bool:
371:    """Check if a ship has functional warp capability.
372:
373:    A ship is warp-capable if:
374:    1. It has a WarpJump ability with max_tonnage >= ship's mass
375:    2. The warp drive is undamaged (warp_max_tonnage will be 0 if damaged)
376:    3. The ship has enough resource storage capacity for at least one warp jump
377:
378:    Args:
379:        ship: Object with get_calculated_stats() method (e.g., ShipInstance)
380:
381:    Returns:
382:        True if ship can use warp points, False otherwise
383:    """
384:    calculated_stats = ship.get_calculated_stats()
385:
386:    ship_mass = calculated_stats.get('mass', 0)
387:    if ship_mass <= 0:
388:        return False
389:
390:    warp_max_tonnage = calculated_stats.get('warp_max_tonnage', 0)
391:    if warp_max_tonnage < ship_mass:
392:        return False
393:
394:    warp_resource_costs = calculated_stats.get('warp_resource_costs', {})
395:    resource_storage = calculated_stats.get('resource_storage', {})
396:
397:    for resource_type, cost in warp_resource_costs.items():
398:        if cost > 0:
399:            capacity = resource_storage.get(resource_type, 0)
400:            if capacity < cost:
401:                return False
402:
403:    return True

45:@dataclass
46:class ShipInstance:
47:    """
48:    Represents a ship in the strategy layer.
49:
50:    Bridges between:
51:    - Ship designs (templates from Ship Builder)
52:    - Strategy fleet management
53:    - Battle simulation
54:
55:    PROJ-438 Phase 3 categorization (post-PROJ-436 Phase 9). The class
56:    is intentionally large because of D2 default (a) â€” keep inline
57:    ``design_data`` and the explicitly-retained shim entry points
58:    documented in PROJ-425 Phase 5d/5e. The 910-caller entry-point sweep
59:    is OUT of PROJ-438 scope. Ratchets live at
60:    ``tests/unit/strategy/ship_instance/test_post_container_surface.py``.
61:
62:    **Owned identity** (immutable across the ship's life):
63:
64:        - ``instance_id`` (uuid), ``design_id``, ``name``, ``owner_id``,
65:          ``serial`` (per-design-per-empire)
66:
67:    **Owned durable state** (inline by design):
68:
69:        - ``design_data``  â€” full serialized ship template; D2 (a) keeps
70:          this inline instead of design-lookup-by-id
71:
72:    **Owned runtime state** (private storage backing the manager APIs):
73:
74:        - ``_consumable_levels`` / ``_cargo_contents``  â€” private dicts;
75:          backward-compat ``consumable_levels`` / ``cargo_contents``
76:          properties below are the read view; canonical writes route
77:          through ``_resource_mgr`` / ``_cargo_mgr``
78:        - ``bay_inventory``  â€” typed two-slot carried inventory (replaces
79:          legacy ``carried_items`` per PROJ-431 Phase 1f; legacy
80:          property + test shim deleted in PROJ-436 Phase 9)
81:        - ``component_toggles``, ``activation_states``, ``components``
82:        - ``current_hp``, ``experience``, ``kills``, ``battles_survived``
83:
84:    **Status flags**:
85:
86:        - ``is_alive``, ``is_derelict``, ``is_operational``,
87:          ``design_role`` / ``role_override``
88:
89:    **Cached / DI slots** (not durable state):
90:
91:        - ``_cached_stats``  â€” invalidated on damage / repair / DI changes
92:        - ``_registries``    â€” injected via ``set_registries``
93:
94:    **Delegate manager slots** (PROJ-425 Phase 4 canonical names):
95:
96:        - ``_resource_mgr`` (``ShipConsumableManager``)
97:        - ``_cargo_mgr`` (``ShipCargoManager``)
98:        - ``_display_fmt`` (``ShipDisplayFormatter``)
99:        - ``_bridge`` (``ShipInstanceBridge``)
100:
101:    **Protocol-alias properties** (``IShipInstance`` compliance, PROJ-193):
102:
103:        - ``design_name``, ``hull_class``, ``ship_name``, ``serial_number``,
104:          ``effective_role``
105:
106:    **Retained-shim entry points** (explicitly kept, NOT to be removed
107:    without the 910-caller sweep â€” PROJ-425 Phase 5d/5e):
108:
109:        - **Serializer shims**: ``to_dict``, ``from_dict``, ``to_json``,
110:          ``from_json``, ``clone``  (~18 callers)
111:        - **Bridge shims**: ``to_ship``, ``update_from_ship``  (~10
112:          callers)
113:        - **Resource-manager shims**: ``consume_resource``,
114:          ``get_resource_capacity``, ``get_current_resource``,
115:          ``get_all_resource_costs_per_hex``,
116:          ``get_all_resource_costs_per_turn``, ``get_warp_resource_costs``,
117:          ``resupply``
118:        - **Write-service shims**: ``set_component_enabled``, ``repair``
119:        - **Backward-compat property shims**: ``consumable_levels`` /
120:          ``cargo_contents`` (post-Phase-3f read view); ``carried_items``
121:          removed in PROJ-436 Phase 9
122:        - **Legacy-kwargs constructor wrapper**:
123:          ``_ship_instance_init_with_legacy_kwargs`` at module scope â€”
124:          retained per PROJ-443 Phase 5b after 18-file audit
125:    """
126:
127:    instance_id: str  # Unique across game
128:    design_id: str    # Reference to ship design file/name
129:    name: str         # Instance name (may differ from design)
130:    owner_id: int     # Empire that owns this ship
131:
132:    # Design data (full serialized ship template)
133:    design_data: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
134:
135:    # Current state (may differ from design defaults)
136:    # None values mean "use design default"
137:    current_hp: Optional[int] = None
138:
139:    # PROJ-436 Phase 3f: ``consumable_levels`` and ``cargo_contents``
140:    # are no longer dataclass fields. They survive as backward-compatible
141:    # write-through ``@property`` accessors over the private
142:    # ``_consumable_levels`` / ``_cargo_contents`` dict fields below
143:    # â€” preserving test infrastructure that still pokes
144:    # ``ship.cargo_contents[k] = v`` / ``ship.consumable_levels[k] = v``
145:    # directly. Production callers route through ``ship._resource_mgr``
146:    # and ``ship._cargo_mgr`` (the stable manager API landed in
147:    # sub-phase 3b). The AST guard at
148:    # ``tests/static_guards/test_no_legacy_storage_fields.py`` pins
149:    # the absence of the dataclass field names.
150:    _consumable_levels: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
151:    component_toggles: Dict[str, bool] = field(default_factory=dict)  # component_id -> enabled
152:    activation_states: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)  # component_key -> activation state
153:
154:    # Per-component-instance persistent state. Key format:
155:    # `component_state_key(component_id, instance_index)` =
156:    # `"{component_id}#{instance_index}"`. Authoritative source for
157:    # battle round-trip (BattleSpec.components / BattleOutcome.components)
158:    # and for per-instance HP in stat calculation. PROJ-269 Phase 2 +
159:    # PROJ-276 (closed the transition; removed the legacy `component_damage`
160:    # dict).
161:    components: Dict[str, ComponentState] = field(default_factory=dict)
162:
163:    # PROJ-436 Phase 3f: see comment on ``_consumable_levels`` above.
164:    _cargo_contents: Dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
165:
166:    # PROJ-431 Phase 1f: typed two-slot carried inventory replaces the
167:    # legacy ``carried_items: List[Dict[str, Any]]`` mixed-shape list.
168:    #
169:    # ``bay_inventory.bay`` holds homogeneous ``CarriedVehicle`` entries
170:    # (mines / fighters / satellites). ``bay_inventory.pods`` holds
171:    # ``DropPod`` entries. The previous runtime
172:    # ``CarriedVehicle.from_any(...)`` discriminator was needed only to
173:    # walk the mixed-shape list and is now removed.
174:    #
175:    # A backward-compatible ``carried_items`` *property* (NOT a dataclass
176:    # field) is kept below for test infrastructure that still pokes the
177:    # legacy dict-list shape â€” see ``carried_items`` property.
178:    bay_inventory: BayInventory = field(default_factory=BayInventory)
179:
180:    # Status
181:    is_alive: bool = True
182:    is_derelict: bool = False
183:    is_operational: bool = True
184:
185:    # Design role classification
186:    design_role: Optional[str] = None    # Auto-classified role (DesignRole value)
187:    role_override: Optional[str] = None  # Player override (DesignRole value)
188:
189:    # Strategy tracking
190:    experience: int = 0           # For future crew/veteran system
191:    kills: int = 0
192:    battles_survived: int = 0
193:
194:    # Serial number - unique per design within an empire
195:    serial: Optional[int] = None
196:
197:    # Cached calculated stats (invalidated on damage change)
198:    _cached_stats: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = field(default=None, repr=False)
199:
200:    # PROJ-211: Injected registries for stats calculation (no global fallback)
201:    _registries: Optional['GameRegistries'] = field(default=None, repr=False, init=False)
202:
203:    # Delegate managers (initialized in __post_init__).
204:    # PROJ-425 Phase 4: canonical names on the entity are `_resource_mgr`
205:    # / `_cargo_mgr` / `_display_fmt` / `_bridge`. The write service was
206:    # querying `_cargo_manager` / `_consumable_manager` (dead code, since
207:    # those attributes never existed); fixed in this phase to match the
208:    # entity instead of renaming ~50 callers across production + tests.
209:    _resource_mgr: Optional['ShipConsumableManager'] = field(default=None, repr=False, init=False)
210:    _cargo_mgr: Optional['ShipCargoManager'] = field(default=None, repr=False, init=False)
211:    _display_fmt: Optional['ShipDisplayFormatter'] = field(default=None, repr=False, init=False)
212:    _bridge: Optional['ShipInstanceBridge'] = field(default=None, repr=False, init=False)
213:
214:    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
215:        """Initialize delegate managers after dataclass init."""
216:        self._resource_mgr = ShipConsumableManager(self)
217:        self._cargo_mgr = ShipCargoManager(self)
218:        self._display_fmt = ShipDisplayFormatter(self)
219:        self._bridge = ShipInstanceBridge(self)
220:
221:    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
222:    # PROJ-436 Phase 3f legacy-shim properties for ``consumable_levels``
223:    # and ``cargo_contents``.
224:    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
225:    #
226:    # The dataclass fields of the same name were deleted in Phase 3f.
227:    # Production code routes through the stable manager APIs
228:    # (``self._resource_mgr.set_level`` / ``get_all_levels`` /
229:    # ``get_current_resource`` / ``replace_levels`` and
230:    # ``self._cargo_mgr.set_cargo`` / ``get_all_cargo`` /
231:    # ``total_cargo_units`` / ``has_cargo``). These properties expose
232:    # the underlying private dicts directly so test infrastructure that
233:    # still does ``ship.consumable_levels[k] = v`` / ``ship.cargo_contents[k] = v``
234:    # /``ship.consumable_levels = {...}`` keeps working without a
235:    # per-test migration.
236:
237:    @property
238:    def consumable_levels(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
239:        """Backward-compatible dict view over private consumable storage.
240:
241:        Phase 3f deletion shim. The dataclass field is gone; the AST
242:        guard at ``tests/static_guards/test_no_legacy_storage_fields.py``
243:        pins the absence. Manager methods read / write the same
244:        private dict, so changes via either API are visible everywhere.
245:        """
246:        return self._consumable_levels
247:
248:    @consumable_levels.setter
249:    def consumable_levels(self, value: Dict[str, float]) -> None:
250:        self._consumable_levels = dict(value) if value is not None else {}
251:
252:    @property
253:    def cargo_contents(self) -> Dict[str, int]:
254:        """Backward-compatible dict view over private cargo storage.
255:
256:        Phase 3f deletion shim. See ``consumable_levels`` property.
257:        """
258:        return self._cargo_contents
259:
260:    @cargo_contents.setter
261:    def cargo_contents(self, value: Dict[str, int]) -> None:
262:        self._cargo_contents = dict(value) if value is not None else {}
263:
264:    def set_registries(self, registries: 'GameRegistries') -> None:
265:        """
266:        Set the registries for stats calculation.
267:
268:        PROJ-211: Allows setting registries after construction for objects
269:        created without registries (e.g., deserialization).
270:
271:        Args:
272:            registries: GameRegistries instance for stats calculation.
273:        """
274:        self._registries = registries
275:        self.invalidate_stats_cache()
276:
277:    # PROJ-193: Property aliases for IShipInstance Protocol compliance
278:    @property
279:    def design_name(self) -> str:
280:        """Design name from design_data (IShipInstance Protocol)."""
281:        return self.design_data.get('name', self.design_id)
282:
283:    @property
284:    def hull_class(self) -> str:
285:        """Ship's hull class from design_data (IShipInstance Protocol)."""
286:        return self.design_data.get('ship_class', 'Unknown')
287:
288:    @property
289:    def ship_name(self) -> str:
290:        """Instance name alias (IShipInstance Protocol)."""
291:        return self.name
292:
293:    @property
294:    def serial_number(self) -> Optional[int]:
295:        """Serial number alias (IShipInstance Protocol)."""
296:        return self.serial
297:
298:    @property
299:    def effective_role(self) -> Optional[str]:
300:        """The active role: role_override if set, else design_role."""
301:        if self.role_override is not None:
302:            return self.role_override
303:        return self.design_role
304:
305:    def __hash__(self) -> int:
306:        return hash(self.instance_id)
307:
308:    def __eq__(self, other: object) -> bool:
309:        if not isinstance(other, ShipInstance):
310:            return NotImplemented
311:        return self.instance_id == other.instance_id
312:
313:    @classmethod
314:    def create(
315:        cls,
316:        design_data: Dict[str, Any],
317:        owner_id: int,
318:        name: Optional[str] = None,
319:        design_id: Optional[str] = None,
320:        empire: Optional['Empire'] = None,
321:        registries: Optional['GameRegistries'] = None,
322:    ) -> 'ShipInstance':
323:        """Create a new ship instance from a design.
324:
325:        Thin shim around `ShipInstanceFactory.create` (PROJ-425 Phase 3).
326:        The shim remains until grep shows no callers still using
327:        `ShipInstance.create(...)` directly.
328:
329:        Args:
330:            design_data: Full ship design dictionary
331:                (from `ShipSerializer.to_dict()`).
332:            owner_id: Empire that owns this ship.
333:            name: Instance name (defaults to design name).
334:            design_id: Design identifier (defaults to design name).
335:            empire: Empire to draw the serial number from. If None, no
336:                serial is assigned and a warning is logged.
337:            registries: `GameRegistries` for stats calculation. Required
338:                for proper DI. Without it, `get_calculated_stats()` raises.
339:
340:        Returns:
341:            New `ShipInstance` with unique `instance_id`.
342:        """
343:        from game.strategy.services.ship_instance_factory import ShipInstanceFactory
344:        return ShipInstanceFactory.create(
345:            design_data=design_data,
346:            owner_id=owner_id,
347:            name=name,
348:            design_id=design_id,
349:            empire=empire,
350:            registries=registries,
351:        )
352:
353:    def is_damaged(self) -> bool:
354:        """Check if ship has any damage â€” hull, per-component, or derelict."""
355:        return (
356:            self.current_hp is not None or
357:            any(cs.is_damaged for cs in self.components.values()) or
358:            self.is_derelict
359:        )
360:
361:    def is_combat_capable(self) -> bool:
362:        """Check if ship can participate in combat."""
363:        return self.is_alive and not self.is_derelict
364:
365:    def get_activation_state(self, component_key: str) -> 'ComponentActivationState':
366:        """Get the activation state for a component."""
367:        from game.strategy.data.component_activation_state import ComponentActivationState
368:        data = self.activation_states.get(component_key)
369:        if data is None:
370:            return ComponentActivationState()
371:        if isinstance(data, dict):
372:            return ComponentActivationState.from_dict(data)
373:        return ComponentActivationState()
374:
375:    def set_activation_state(self, component_key: str, state: 'ComponentActivationState') -> None:
376:        """Store the activation state for a component."""
377:        self.activation_states[component_key] = state.to_dict()
378:
379:    def get_calculated_stats(self, force_refresh: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:
380:        """
381:        Get calculated stats from components, respecting damage state.
382:
383:        Delegates to `ShipStatsCache` (PROJ-425 Phase 1): the helper owns
384:        the registry-DI calculation path and the cache rule. Storage of
385:        `_cached_stats` remains on this entity per TD-06 Guardrail #2.
386:
387:        Args:
388:            force_refresh: If True, recalculate even if cached
389:
390:        Returns:
391:            Dict with calculated stats (max_hp, mass, resource_storage, etc.)
392:        """
393:        from game.strategy.data.ship_stats_cache import ShipStatsCache
394:        return ShipStatsCache.get_or_compute(self, force_refresh=force_refresh)
395:
396:    def invalidate_stats_cache(self) -> None:
397:        """
398:        Invalidate cached stats.
399:
400:        Call this when component damage changes, after battle,
401:        or after repair operations.
402:        """
403:        from game.strategy.data.ship_stats_cache import ShipStatsCache
404:        ShipStatsCache.invalidate(self)
405:
406:    def get_hp_percentage(self) -> float:
407:        """Get current HP as percentage of max."""
408:        if self.current_hp is None:
409:            return 1.0
410:        max_hp = self.get_calculated_stats().get('max_hp', _DEFAULT_MAX_HP)
411:        if max_hp <= 0:
412:            return 0.0
413:        return self.current_hp / max_hp
414:
415:    def get_resource_percentage(self, resource_name: str) -> float:
416:        """Get current resource level as percentage of max."""
417:        return self._display_fmt.get_resource_percentage(resource_name)
418:
419:    # --- Generic Resource Methods (PROJ-425 Phase 5b: thin shims) ---
420:    #
421:    # These remain as forwarders because too many tests mock them as
422:    # entity-level methods (e.g. `ship.consume_resource = Mock(...)`).
423:    # Migrating those mocks is a bigger change than the demolition saves.
424:    # The canonical implementations live on `ShipConsumableManager`.
425:
426:    def get_resource_capacity(self, resource_type: str) -> float:
427:        """Get maximum capacity for a resource type (shim to `_resource_mgr`)."""
428:        return self._resource_mgr.get_resource_capacity(resource_type)
429:
430:    def get_current_resource(self, resource_type: str) -> float:
431:        """Get current level of a resource type (shim to `_resource_mgr`)."""
432:        return self._resource_mgr.get_current_resource(resource_type)
433:
434:    def consume_resource(self, resource_type: str, amount: float) -> bool:
435:        """Consume a resource amount (shim to `_resource_mgr`)."""
436:        return self._resource_mgr.consume_resource(resource_type, amount)
437:
438:    def get_all_resource_costs_per_hex(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
439:        """Per-hex consumption costs (shim to `_resource_mgr`)."""
440:        return self._resource_mgr.get_all_resource_costs_per_hex()
441:
442:    def get_all_resource_costs_per_turn(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
443:        """Per-turn consumption costs (shim to `_resource_mgr`)."""
444:        return self._resource_mgr.get_all_resource_costs_per_turn()
445:
446:    # --- Cargo / carried-vehicle helpers ---
447:    #
448:    # PROJ-425 Phase 6 (TD-06 batch 5c): the method forwarders for
449:    # cargo queries / mutators, carried-vehicle queries, and pod-storage
450:    # helpers were demolished. Callers go through ``ship._cargo_mgr``
451:    # (see :class:`ShipCargoManager`). The ``bay_current_mass`` and
452:    # ``bay_capacity_mass`` *properties* below are kept as small
453:    # read-only entity attributes â€” they read naturally as
454:    # ship-instance state (parallel to ``design_name`` /
455:    # ``hull_class``) and are referenced by tests as such.
456:
457:    @property
458:    def bay_current_mass(self) -> float:
459:        """PROJ-FMS-A: runtime vehicle-bay usage on this ship.
460:
461:        Mirrors the ``bay_capacity_mass`` design stat. Computed at the
462:        strategy layer because it depends on the actual contents of
463:        ``bay_inventory.bay`` (simulation ``Ship`` cannot see those).
464:        """
465:        return self._cargo_mgr.get_carried_vehicle_mass()
466:
467:    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
468:    # PROJ-436 Phase 3a â€” unified Container projections.
469:    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
470:    #
471:    # These are read-only snapshot views over the legacy
472:    # `consumable_levels` and `cargo_contents` dataclass fields. Later
473:    # sub-phases of Phase 3 migrate callers off the legacy fields; the
474:    # final cutover deletes both fields and these views become the
475:    # canonical read API. Until then, the views serve callers that
476:    # want to work in the unified Container abstraction without
477:    # forcing a wholesale migration.
478:    #
479:    # Snapshot semantics: mutations on the returned Container are NOT
480:    # propagated back to the ship. Treat as a read view.
481:
482:    def consumable_container(self) -> 'Container':
483:        """Container snapshot of this ship's `consumable_levels`.
484:
485:        Every entry in `consumable_levels` (resource_name -> amount)
486:        appears in the resource slice. Unknown resource ids (not in
487:        `ResourceCatalog`) raise via `Container.add_resource` â€”
488:        consumable_levels keys are assumed to be canonical resource ids
489:        per the existing component-driven loading code.
490:        """
491:        from game.strategy.data.container import (
492:            Container,
493:            ContainerPolicy,
494:        )
495:        from game.strategy.data.containable import (
496:            ContainableKind,
497:            ResourceContainable,
498:        )
499:
500:        policy = ContainerPolicy(
501:            allowed_kinds=frozenset({ContainableKind.RESOURCE}),
502:            allowed_type_ids=None,
503:        )
504:        c = Container(capacity_mass=float("inf"), policy=policy)
505:        for resource_id, amount in self.consumable_levels.items():
506:            c.add(ResourceContainable(resource_id), amount)
507:        return c
508:
509:    def cargo_container(self) -> 'Container':
510:        """Container snapshot of this ship's `cargo_contents`.
511:
512:        Translation rules for cargo_contents keys:
513:        - "passengers" -> population slice (default species id).
514:        - Known resource ids (in `ResourceCatalog`) -> resource slice.
515:        - Other keys -> logged and skipped (no representation in the
516:          snapshot). The most common skipped keys are "drop_pod" and
517:          "vehicle" which live in `bay_inventory` instead.
518:        """
519:        from game.core.resources import ResourceCatalog
520:        from game.strategy.data.container import (
521:            Container,
522:            ContainerPolicy,
523:            _get_resource_catalog,
524:        )
525:        from game.strategy.data.containable import (
526:            ContainableKind,
527:            PopulationContainable,
528:            ResourceContainable,
529:        )
530:
531:        catalog: ResourceCatalog = _get_resource_catalog()
532:        policy = ContainerPolicy(
533:            allowed_kinds=frozenset({
534:                ContainableKind.RESOURCE,
535:                ContainableKind.POPULATION,
536:            }),
537:            allowed_type_ids=None,
538:        )
539:        c = Container(capacity_mass=float("inf"), policy=policy)
540:        for key, amount in self.cargo_contents.items():
541:            if key == "passengers":
542:                c.add(PopulationContainable("default"), int(amount))
543:            elif catalog.has(key):
544:                c.add(ResourceContainable(key), float(amount))
545:            else:
546:                logger.debug(
547:                    "cargo_container: skipping cargo_contents key %r "
548:                    "(not a known resource id or 'passengers')",
549:                    key,
550:                )
551:        return c
552:
553:    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
554:    # PROJ-431 Phase 1f: bay_inventory IS the canonical storage.
555:    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
556:    #
557:    # The typed :class:`BayInventory` (``bay: list[CarriedVehicle]`` +
558:    # ``pods: list[DropPod]``) is now a real dataclass field on this
559:    # entity, not a projection over the legacy mixed-shape list. The
560:    # earlier ``carried_items`` field was removed; what remains here is
561:    # a backward-compatible property/setter exposing the legacy
562:    # dict-list shape exclusively for **test infrastructure** that still
563:    # pokes ``ship.carried_items.append({...})`` directly. Production
564:    # code uses :attr:`bay_inventory` and :meth:`set_bay_inventory`.
565:
566:    def set_bay_inventory(self, bay_inventory: 'BayInventory') -> None:
567:        """Replace this ship's typed bay inventory wholesale.
568:
569:        Phase 1f: this is a thin attribute setter retained as a stable
570:        write surface for callers that produced a new
571:        :class:`BayInventory` value (e.g. ``ShipCargoManager`` loaders /
572:        unloaders, FMS handlers). Callers that just mutate the lists in
573:        place do not need to call this â€” the underlying field is now
574:        owned by this entity directly.
575:        """
576:        if not isinstance(bay_inventory, BayInventory):
577:            raise TypeError(
578:                f"set_bay_inventory expects BayInventory, got "
579:                f"{type(bay_inventory).__name__}"
580:            )
581:        self.bay_inventory = bay_inventory
582:
583:    # PROJ-436 Phase 9: ``carried_items`` property + ``_CarriedItemsProxy``
584:    # test shim deleted. Callers route through ``bay_inventory.bay`` /
585:    # ``bay_inventory.pods`` / ``set_bay_inventory(...)`` directly.
586:
587:    @property
588:    def bay_capacity_mass(self) -> float:
589:        """PROJ-FMS-A: maximum vehicle-bay capacity from design stats."""
590:        _current, max_mass = self._cargo_mgr.get_vehicle_bay_capacity()
591:        return max_mass
592:
593:    # PROJ-425 Phase 6 (TD-06 batch 5c): pod-storage helpers
594:    # (``get_pod_storage_capacity`` / ``get_pod_storage_used`` /
595:    # ``can_carry_pod``) were demolished; callers go through
596:    # ``ship._cargo_mgr`` directly.
597:
598:    def get_warp_resource_costs(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
599:        """Warp jump resource costs (shim to `_resource_mgr`)."""
600:        return self._resource_mgr.get_warp_resource_costs()

789:# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
790:# PROJ-436 Phase 3f: legacy-kwarg constructor wrapper.
791:# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
792:# The dataclass field rename (``consumable_levels`` ->
793:# ``_consumable_levels``, ``cargo_contents`` -> ``_cargo_contents``)
794:# would break ~18 test files that pass the legacy kwarg names into
795:# ``ShipInstance(...)``. Rather than sweep those mechanically, we
796:# wrap the dataclass-generated ``__init__`` with a translator that
797:# accepts both spellings. Production code never hits the translation
798:# branch (the serializer / clone paths use the new private names).
799:#
800:# PROJ-443 Phase 5b retained-with-rationale: an initial sweep attempt
801:# found the real test-side footprint is 18 files (the original PROJ-436
802:# audit estimated ~7). The wrapper is small (~20 LOC), production
803:# carries no runtime cost (kwargs are not the production path), and
804:# the cleanup value does not justify the change to 18 test files. See
805:# `Projects/active_projects/PROJ-443/decisions.md` 2026-05-17 row
806:# "Phase 5b implementation: retained wrapper after audit found 18
807:# files (not ~7)".
808:
809:_dataclass_init = ShipInstance.__init__
810:
811:
812:def _ship_instance_init_with_legacy_kwargs(self, *args, **kwargs):  # noqa: D401
813:    """Translate legacy ``consumable_levels`` / ``cargo_contents`` kwargs.
814:
815:    PROJ-436 Phase 3f compat shim. Production callers pass the new
816:    private-field names (``_consumable_levels`` / ``_cargo_contents``);
817:    only legacy test fixtures hit the translation branch.
818:    """
819:    if 'consumable_levels' in kwargs:
820:        if '_consumable_levels' in kwargs:
821:            raise TypeError(
822:                "ShipInstance.__init__ received both 'consumable_levels' "
823:                "and '_consumable_levels'; pass only one."
824:            )
825:        kwargs['_consumable_levels'] = kwargs.pop('consumable_levels')
826:    if 'cargo_contents' in kwargs:
827:        if '_cargo_contents' in kwargs:
828:            raise TypeError(
829:                "ShipInstance.__init__ received both 'cargo_contents' "
830:                "and '_cargo_contents'; pass only one."
831:            )
832:        kwargs['_cargo_contents'] = kwargs.pop('cargo_contents')
833:    _dataclass_init(self, *args, **kwargs)

1:"""ColonizeHandler -- handles `OrderType.COLONIZE` (PROJ-368 Phase 2).
2:
3:Lifted from `OrderProcessor.process_colonize` (lines 151-249) and
4:`OrderProcessor._deploy_drop_pod` (lines 618-652). Phase 2 Rework
5:(retained from PROJ-238): colony pods are cargo items consumed at
6:colonization; the carrying ship is reusable and stays in the fleet.
7:
8:PROJ-431 Phase 1d: drop-pod deploy walks ``ship.bay_inventory.pods``
9:(typed ``DropPod`` entries) instead of the legacy mixed-shape
10:``carried_items`` list. The consumed pod is removed by rebuilding
11:``ship.bay_inventory`` with that pod dropped and calling
12:``ship.set_bay_inventory(...)``.
13:
14:Q1 resolution (decisions.md): missing `component_registry` continues to
15:log+pop+False (preserved backward-compat). Stronger ValueError contract
16:deferred to a future tech-debt project.

18:from __future__ import annotations

20:from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, TYPE_CHECKING, Tuple

23:from game.strategy.data.fleet import Fleet
24:from game.strategy.data.order_types import OrderType

26:    BaseOrderHandler,

31:logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

33:if TYPE_CHECKING:
34:    from game.strategy.data.empire import Empire
35:    from game.strategy.data.galaxy import Galaxy

38:class ColonizeHandler(BaseOrderHandler):
39:    """Handler for `OrderType.COLONIZE`."""
40:
41:    @property
42:    def supported_order_types(self) -> Tuple[OrderType, ...]:
43:        return (OrderType.COLONIZE,)
44:
45:    def execute_action_order(
46:        self,
47:        fleet: Fleet,
48:        empire: "Empire",
49:        galaxy: "Galaxy",
50:        component_registry: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
51:        empires: Optional[List["Empire"]] = None,
52:    ) -> OrderExecutionResult:
53:        """Execute a COLONIZE order. Lift-and-shift of `process_colonize`."""
54:        from game.strategy.validation import ColonizeValidator
55:
56:        order = fleet.get_current_order()
57:        if not order or order.type != OrderType.COLONIZE:
58:            return OrderExecutionResult(success=False, colonized=False)
59:
60:        if component_registry is None:
61:            # Q1 resolution: preserve log+pop+False for backward compat.
62:            logger.error("ColonizeHandler: COLONIZE order requires component_registry")
63:            fleet.pop_order()
64:            return OrderExecutionResult(success=False, colonized=False)
65:
66:        # Extract target planet (may be a plain Planet or a dict with planet key)
67:        raw_target = order.target
68:        if isinstance(raw_target, dict):
69:            target_planet = raw_target.get('planet')
70:        else:
71:            target_planet = raw_target
72:
73:        # Validate (skip chain check -- we're executing, not adding)
74:        validation = ColonizeValidator.validate(
75:            galaxy, fleet, target_planet, component_registry, skip_chain_check=True
76:        )
77:        if not validation.is_valid:
78:            logger.warning(f"ColonizeHandler: Colonize failed - {validation.message}")
79:            fleet.pop_order()
80:            return OrderExecutionResult(success=False, colonized=False)
81:
82:        # Determine final planet (for "Any" case, pick first unowned)
83:        if target_planet is not None:
84:            final_planet = target_planet
85:        else:
86:            planets_at_loc = galaxy.get_planets_at_global_hex(fleet.location)
87:            valid_candidates = [p for p in planets_at_loc if p.owner_id is None]
88:            final_planet = valid_candidates[0] if valid_candidates else None
89:
90:            if final_planet is None:
91:                logger.warning("ColonizeHandler: No candidate planet for colonization")
92:                fleet.pop_order()
93:                return OrderExecutionResult(success=False, colonized=False)
94:
95:        # Pre-check drop pod availability BEFORE any mutation
96:        if not ColonizeValidator.fleet_has_drop_pod(fleet):
97:            logger.warning("ColonizeHandler: No drop pod in fleet")
98:            fleet.pop_order()
99:            return OrderExecutionResult(success=False, colonized=False)
100:
101:        # Execute colonization -- claim planet and deploy pod only.
102:        # Population and cargo transfer is handled by TRANSFER orders queued after COLONIZE.
103:        empire.add_colony(final_planet)
104:        fleet.pop_order()
105:
106:        # Deploy drop pod as facility on the new colony
107:        self._deploy_drop_pod(fleet, final_planet, empire=empire)
108:
109:        logger.info(f"ColonizeHandler: Colonization successful. {empire.name} claimed {final_planet.name}")
110:
111:        # Look up system name and local hex for granular event log columns
112:        system_name = ""
113:        local_hex = None
114:        if galaxy and hasattr(galaxy, 'get_system_of_planet'):
115:            sys = galaxy.get_system_of_planet(final_planet)
116:            if sys:
117:                system_name = sys.name
118:                if hasattr(final_planet, 'location') and final_planet.location is not None:
119:                    local_hex = [final_planet.location.q, final_planet.location.r]
120:
121:        self._emit_event(
122:            EventType.COLONY_FOUNDED,
123:            category=EventCategory.COLONIES,
124:            empire_id=empire.id,
125:            message=f"Founded colony on {final_planet.name}",
126:            planet_id=final_planet.id,
127:            planet_name=final_planet.name,
128:            fleet_id=fleet.id,
129:            location_name=final_planet.name,
130:            location_hex=[fleet.location.q, fleet.location.r],
131:            system_name=system_name,
132:            local_hex=local_hex,
133:        )
134:        # PROJ-368: legacy execute_action_order's COLONIZE branch returned
135:        # `result.colonized` as the "fleet consumed" signal -- documented
136:        # quirk preserved for backward compat with existing characterization
137:        # tests in test_fleet_order_processor.py.
138:        return OrderExecutionResult(
139:            success=True,
140:            fleet_consumed=True,  # mirror legacy: colonized -> fleet_consumed
141:            colonized=True,
142:            planet_name=final_planet.name,
143:        )
144:
145:    def _deploy_drop_pod(
146:        self, fleet: Fleet, planet: Any, *, empire: Optional["Empire"] = None,
147:    ) -> None:
148:        """Deploy a drop pod from fleet cargo as a facility on the planet.
149:
150:        PROJ-431 Phase 1d: finds the first drop pod in any ship's
151:        ``bay_inventory.pods``, removes it via ``set_bay_inventory(...)``
152:        (rebuilding the inventory with that pod dropped), and creates a
153:        PlanetaryFacility from its ``design_data``. The full design (all
154:        components the player chose) becomes the facility.
155:
156:        PROJ-412 Phase 5: ``empire`` is forwarded to ``add_facility`` so
157:        the empire's storage/booster dirty flags flip mid-turn.
158:        """
159:        from uuid import uuid4
160:        from game.strategy.data.bay_inventory import BayInventory
161:        from game.strategy.data.planetary_facility import PlanetaryFacility
162:        from game.strategy.validation.colonize_validator import ColonizeValidator
163:
164:        ship, pod_index = ColonizeValidator.find_ship_with_drop_pod(fleet)
165:        if ship is None:
166:            logger.warning("_deploy_drop_pod: No drop pod found in fleet")
167:            return
168:
169:        # Remove the drop pod from the ship by rebuilding bay_inventory
170:        # without that pod. PROJ-431 Phase 1d: typed write-through.
171:        current_bay = ship.bay_inventory
172:        if pod_index < 0 or pod_index >= len(current_bay.pods):
173:            logger.warning(
174:                "_deploy_drop_pod: pod_index %d out of range (pods=%d)",
175:                pod_index,
176:                len(current_bay.pods),
177:            )
178:            return
179:        drop_pod = current_bay.pods[pod_index]
180:        remaining_pods = [
181:            p for i, p in enumerate(current_bay.pods) if i != pod_index
182:        ]
183:        ship.set_bay_inventory(
184:            BayInventory(bay=list(current_bay.bay), pods=remaining_pods)
185:        )
186:
187:        design_data = drop_pod.design_data or {}
188:        # The legacy entry preserved a 'name' field in its payload. The
189:        # typed ``DropPod`` keeps payload-shape extras opaquely so the
190:        # facility name continues to round-trip through the same slot.
191:        pod_name = drop_pod.payload.get("name", "Colony Drop Pod")
192:
193:        facility = PlanetaryFacility(
194:            instance_id=uuid4().hex,
195:            design_id=drop_pod.design_id or "drop_pod",
196:            name=pod_name,
197:            design_data=design_data,
198:            is_operational=True,
199:        )
200:        # PROJ-370 Phase 3: route through IPlanetMutator.
201:        # PROJ-412 Phase 5: forward empire so HarvestingEngine's per-turn
202:        # storage/booster caches see the new facility mid-turn.
203:        self._get_planet_mutator().add_facility(planet, facility, empire=empire)
204:
205:        # Seed planet stockpile from design's initial_stockpile if present
206:        initial_stock = design_data.get("initial_stockpile", {})
207:        for resource, amount in initial_stock.items():
208:            planet.add_to_stockpile(resource, float(amount))
209:
210:        logger.info(f"Deployed drop pod '{facility.name}' on {planet.name}")

1:"""TransferHandler -- handles `OrderType.{TRANSFER, LOAD_POPULATION,
2:UNLOAD_POPULATION}` (PROJ-368 Phase 3).
3:
4:The single most complex handler in PROJ-368. Subsumes:
5:- `OrderProcessor.process_transfer` (114 LOC, 5 implicit branches)
6:- `OrderProcessor._execute_load`, `_execute_unload`,
7:  `_execute_fleet_transfer` (legacy private helpers)
8:- `OrderProcessor._load_pod_from_staging_yard`,
9:  `_unload_pod_to_staging_yard`
10:
11:Decomposes the 5 implicit branches in `process_transfer` into 7
12:**explicit** `_dispatch_*` private methods, exposing the cargo-type
13:sub-branches the legacy code conflated:
14:
15:  1. _dispatch_load_planet_resource     (planet, load,   resource cargo)
16:  2. _dispatch_load_planet_passengers   (planet, load,   passengers)
17:  3. _dispatch_drop_pod_load            (planet, load,   drop_pod)
18:  4. _dispatch_unload_planet_resource   (planet, unload, resource cargo)
19:  5. _dispatch_unload_planet_passengers (planet, unload, passengers)
20:  6. _dispatch_drop_pod_unload          (planet, unload, drop_pod)
21:  7. _dispatch_fleet_to_fleet           (fleet target, generic)
22:
23:Three preserved invariants:
24:- BUG-70 LOAD_POPULATION auto-resolve at fleet hex.
25:- PROJ-343 T1.1 target_fleet_id resolution against galaxy.empires
26:  with empire.fleets fallback (resolver brittleness preserved
27:  verbatim; the call-site fix lives in handlers/transfer.py).

29:  this handler; the cargo_type == "drop_pod" path skips the location
30:  check via `skip_location_check`.

52:class TransferHandler(_TransferDispatchMixin, BaseOrderHandler):
53:    """Handler for the TRANSFER family (TRANSFER, LOAD_POPULATION, UNLOAD_POPULATION).
54:
55:    PROJ-368 review MAJ-001: the seven `_dispatch_*` branches live in
56:    `transfer_branches._TransferDispatchMixin` to keep this file under the
57:    500-LOC ceiling ahead of PROJ-370 Phase 3.
58:    """
59:
60:    @property
61:    def supported_order_types(self) -> Tuple[OrderType, ...]:
62:        return (OrderType.TRANSFER, OrderType.LOAD_POPULATION, OrderType.UNLOAD_POPULATION)
63:
64:    def execute_action_order(
65:        self,
66:        fleet: Fleet,
67:        empire: "Empire",
68:        galaxy: "Galaxy",
69:        component_registry: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
70:        empires: Optional[List["Empire"]] = None,
71:    ) -> OrderExecutionResult:
72:        """Execute a TRANSFER / LOAD_POPULATION / UNLOAD_POPULATION order."""
73:        from game.strategy.validation import TransferValidator
74:
75:        order = fleet.get_current_order()
76:        if not order or order.type not in (
77:            OrderType.TRANSFER,
78:            OrderType.LOAD_POPULATION,
79:            OrderType.UNLOAD_POPULATION,
80:        ):
81:            return OrderExecutionResult(
82:                success=False, message="No TRANSFER order"
83:            )
84:
85:        # Extract params from order target dict
86:        params = order.target
87:        if not isinstance(params, dict):
88:            fleet.pop_order()
89:            return OrderExecutionResult(
90:                success=False, message="Invalid transfer params"
91:            )
92:
93:        direction = params.get("direction", "")
94:        cargo_type = params.get("cargo_type", "")
95:        amount = params.get("amount", 0)
96:        planet_id = params.get("planet_id")
97:        target_fleet_id = params.get("target_fleet_id")
98:        species_id = params.get("species_id")
99:
100:        # Resolve target.
101:        target = None
102:        if planet_id:
103:            target = galaxy.get_planet_by_id(planet_id)
104:        elif (
105:            not planet_id
106:            and not target_fleet_id
107:            and order.type == OrderType.LOAD_POPULATION
108:        ):
109:            # BUG-70: Generic LOAD_POPULATION -- auto-resolve colony at fleet's current hex
110:            planets_at_hex = galaxy.get_planets_at_global_hex(fleet.location)
111:            for p in planets_at_hex:
112:                if (
113:                    p.owner_id == empire.id
114:                    and hasattr(p, "populations")
115:                    and p.populations
116:                    and p.total_population > 0
117:                ):
118:                    target = p
119:                    logger.debug(
120:                        f"BUG-70: Auto-resolved colony {p.name} (pop={p.total_population}) at fleet hex {fleet.location}"
121:                    )
122:                    break
123:            if not target:
124:                # No owned colony at fleet hex -- no-op, continue with next order
125:                logger.debug(
126:                    f"BUG-70: No owned colony at fleet hex {fleet.location}, skipping LOAD_POPULATION"
127:                )
128:                fleet.pop_order()
129:                return OrderExecutionResult(
130:                    success=True,
131:                    message="No colony at location, skipped",
132:                )
133:        elif target_fleet_id:
134:            target = self._resolve_target_fleet_by_id(target_fleet_id, empire, galaxy)
135:
136:        # Validate -- skip location check for drop_pod / vehicle (fleet is
137:        # already at planet via MOVE order).
138:        skip_loc = cargo_type in ("drop_pod", "vehicle")
139:        logger.info(
140:            f"TransferHandler.execute_action_order: fleet={fleet.id} cargo={cargo_type} "
141:            f"dir={direction} amt={amount} species={species_id} "
142:            f"target={getattr(target, 'name', target)} skip_loc={skip_loc}"
143:        )
144:        validation = TransferValidator.validate(
145:            galaxy, fleet, target, cargo_type, direction, amount, species_id,
146:            skip_location_check=skip_loc,
147:        )
148:
149:        if not validation.is_valid:
150:            logger.warning(
151:                f"TransferHandler: Transfer failed - {validation.message} "
152:                f"(code={validation.error_code})"
153:            )
154:            fleet.pop_order()
155:            return OrderExecutionResult(
156:                success=False, message=validation.message
157:            )
158:
159:        # Dispatch into one of 7 explicit branches.
160:        from game.core.protocols import is_planet, is_fleet
161:        transferred = 0
162:
163:        if is_planet(target):
164:            if direction == "load":
165:                logger.info(

1:"""JoinFleetHandler â€” handles `OrderType.JOIN_FLEET` (PROJ-368 Phase 1).
2:
3:Owns:
4:    - Single-fleet `process_join_fleet` path (lifted from `OrderProcessor`)
5:    - BUG-122 three-phase `process_instant_orders` pipeline (Phase A
6:      collect, Phase B mutual-pair canonicalize, Phase C aliveness
7:      re-validate + execute)
8:    - Helpers: `_validate_tick_inputs`, `_execute_fleet_merge`,
9:      `_elect_canonical_merges`, `_emit_join_cancelled`
10:
11:Architectural decision (PROJ-368 Phase 1 Task 1.4 â€” resolves
12:`decisions.md` row 8):
13:
14:    `process_instant_orders` is a `JoinFleetHandler`-only public method
15:    (Option B). The facade calls it explicitly via
16:    `registry.get(OrderType.JOIN_FLEET).process_instant_orders(empires)`.
17:    Adding the method to the `IOrderHandler` Protocol with
18:    `NotImplementedError` defaults on the other 4 handlers (Option A)
19:    was rejected: the instant path is JOIN_FLEET-only by design, and
20:    the protocol stays minimal (one method) so non-instant handlers
21:    don't carry a no-op shim.

43:class JoinFleetHandler(BaseOrderHandler):
44:    """Handler for `OrderType.JOIN_FLEET` (single-fleet + instant batch)."""
45:
46:    @property
47:    def supported_order_types(self) -> Tuple[OrderType, ...]:
48:        return (OrderType.JOIN_FLEET,)
49:
50:    def execute_action_order(
51:        self,
52:        fleet: Fleet,
53:        empire: "Empire",
54:        galaxy: "Galaxy",
55:        component_registry: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
56:        empires: Optional[List["Empire"]] = None,
57:    ) -> OrderExecutionResult:
58:        """Process a single JOIN_FLEET order.
59:
60:        Lifted from `OrderProcessor.process_join_fleet` (lines 110-149).
61:        Merges fleet into target if at same location.
62:        """
63:        order = fleet.get_current_order()
64:        if not order or order.type != OrderType.JOIN_FLEET:
65:            return OrderExecutionResult(success=False, merged=False)
66:
67:        target_fleet = order.target
68:
69:        # Validation: target must be a valid Fleet (Fleet always has location)
70:        if target_fleet is None:
71:            logger.warning("JoinFleetHandler: Join Fleet failed - Target invalid/destroyed.")
72:            fleet.pop_order()
73:            return OrderExecutionResult(success=False, merged=False, cancelled=True)
74:
75:        if fleet.location == target_fleet.location:
76:            logger.debug(f"JoinFleetHandler: Fleet {fleet.id} merging into {target_fleet.id}")
77:            self._execute_fleet_merge(fleet, target_fleet, empire)
78:            return OrderExecutionResult(success=True, merged=True)
79:        else:
80:            # Not at location yet
81:            logger.warning("JoinFleetHandler: Join Fleet failed - Not at same location.")
82:            fleet.pop_order()
83:            return OrderExecutionResult(success=False, merged=False)
84:
85:    def process_instant_orders(
86:        self,
87:        empires: List["Empire"],
88:    ) -> List[Tuple["Empire", Fleet]]:
89:        """Process JOIN_FLEET orders for co-located fleets during a tick.
90:
91:        BUG-122: Three-phase implementation with mutual-pair canonicalisation
92:        and per-iteration aliveness re-validation:
93:
94:          Phase A - collect candidate (empire, source, target) tuples.
95:          Phase B - collapse mutual A<->B pairs to a single canonical merge,
96:                    most-ships-wins (smaller id breaks ties). Cycles of 3+
97:                    are NOT pre-collapsed - Phase C's re-validation handles
98:                    them naturally (whichever direction iterates first wins).
99:          Phase C - execute, re-checking that source AND target are still
100:                    in empire.fleets at execution time. Skipped entries fire
101:                    FLEET_JOIN_CANCELLED with a `reason` field.
102:
103:        Args:
104:            empires: List of Empire objects.
105:
106:        Returns:
107:            List of (empire, fleet) tuples for removed fleets.
108:        """
109:        self._validate_tick_inputs(empires)
110:
111:        # Phase A: collect candidates
112:        candidates: List[Tuple["Empire", Fleet, Fleet]] = []
113:        for empire in empires:
114:            for fleet in list(empire.fleets):
115:                order = fleet.get_current_order()
116:                if order and order.type == OrderType.JOIN_FLEET:
117:                    target_fleet = order.target
118:                    if target_fleet is not None and fleet.location == target_fleet.location:
119:                        logger.debug(
120:                            f"JoinFleetHandler [Instant]: Fleet {fleet.id} candidate merge into {target_fleet.id}"
121:                        )
122:                        candidates.append((empire, fleet, target_fleet))
123:
124:        # Phase B: canonicalise mutual pairs (most ships wins, smaller id breaks ties)
125:        canonical = self._elect_canonical_merges(candidates)
126:
127:        # Phase C: execute with re-validation
128:        result: List[Tuple["Empire", Fleet]] = []
129:        for empire, fleet, target_fleet in canonical:
130:            if fleet not in empire.fleets:
131:                logger.warning(
132:                    f"[BUG-122] Skip merge: source Fleet {fleet.id} no longer in "
133:                    f"Empire {empire.id} (absorbed by earlier merge this tick)"
134:                )
135:                self._emit_join_cancelled(
136:                    fleet, target_fleet, empire,
137:                    reason="absorbed_by_other_merge",
138:                )
139:                continue
140:            if target_fleet not in empire.fleets:
141:                logger.warning(
142:                    f"[BUG-122] Skip merge: target Fleet {target_fleet.id} no longer in "
143:                    f"Empire {empire.id} (absorbed mid-iteration)"
144:                )
145:                self._emit_join_cancelled(
146:                    fleet, target_fleet, empire,
147:                    reason="target_absorbed_mid_iteration",
148:                )
149:                # Pop the now-stale JOIN_FLEET order so the source can move on
150:                current = fleet.get_current_order()
151:                if current and current.type == OrderType.JOIN_FLEET and current.target is target_fleet:
152:                    fleet.pop_order()
153:                continue
154:            self._execute_fleet_merge(fleet, target_fleet, empire)
155:            result.append((empire, fleet))
156:
157:        return result
158:
159:    def _validate_tick_inputs(self, empires: List["Empire"]) -> None:
160:        """PROJ-251: Validate preconditions before mutating state."""
161:        from game.core.exceptions import ValidationException
162:        for empire in empires:
163:            for fleet in empire.fleets:
164:                if fleet.orders is None:
165:                    raise ValidationException(
166:                        f"Empire {empire.id}: fleet '{fleet.id}' has None orders list",
167:                        context={"empire_id": empire.id, "fleet_id": fleet.id}
168:                    )
169:
170:    def _execute_fleet_merge(
171:        self,
172:        fleet: Fleet,
173:        target_fleet: Fleet,
174:        empire: "Empire",
175:    ) -> None:
176:        """Merge fleet into target and log the event.
177:
178:        Shared logic for both single-fleet processing (execute_action_order)
179:        and batch processing (process_instant_orders).
180:
181:        Args:
182:            fleet: Fleet being merged (will be removed).
183:            target_fleet: Fleet receiving the merged ships.
184:            empire: Empire that owns both fleets.
185:        """
186:        fleet.merge_with(target_fleet, event_bus=self._event_bus)
187:        empire.remove_fleet(fleet, event_bus=self._event_bus)
188:        self._emit_event(
189:            EventType.FLEET_JOINED,
190:            category=EventCategory.FLEET_OPERATIONS,
191:            empire_id=empire.id,
192:            message=f"Fleet {fleet.id} joined Fleet {target_fleet.id}",
193:            fleet_id=fleet.id,
194:            target_fleet_id=target_fleet.id,
195:            ship_count=len(target_fleet.ships),
196:        )
197:
198:    def _elect_canonical_merges(
199:        self,
200:        candidates: List[Tuple["Empire", Fleet, Fleet]],
201:    ) -> List[Tuple["Empire", Fleet, Fleet]]:
202:        """Collapse mutual A<->B JOIN_FLEET pairs into a single canonical merge.
203:
204:        Election rule (BUG-122 Q2): the fleet with more ships absorbs the
205:        other. On equal ship counts, the smaller fleet id wins (deterministic
206:        for tests). Ship counts are read at election time and do NOT change
207:        during the rest of this tick - the rule is fixed once decided.
208:
209:        Cycles of 3+ are NOT collapsed here; Phase C's per-iteration
210:        aliveness check handles them: whichever direction iterates first
211:        runs, the rest skip with `target_absorbed_mid_iteration`. This
212:        keeps cycle handling simple and parallel to PROJ-275's N-team
213:        battle pattern (no pairwise decomposition).
214:
215:        Args:
216:            candidates: [(empire, source, target), ...] from Phase A.
217:
218:        Returns:
219:            Filtered list with mutual pairs collapsed to one tuple each.
220:        """
221:        # Index candidates by source for O(1) mutual lookup
222:        by_source: Dict[Fleet, Tuple["Empire", Fleet, Fleet]] = {
223:            source: (empire, source, target)
224:            for empire, source, target in candidates
225:        }
226:        result: List[Tuple["Empire", Fleet, Fleet]] = []
227:        consumed: set = set()
228:
229:        for empire, source, target in candidates:
230:            if source in consumed:
231:                continue
232:            mutual = by_source.get(target)
233:            if mutual is not None and mutual[2] is source:
234:                # Mutual pair: source <-> target. Elect the winner.
235:                source_ships = len(source.ships)
236:                target_ships = len(target.ships)
237:                if source_ships > target_ships:
238:                    winner, loser = source, target
239:                elif target_ships > source_ships:
240:                    winner, loser = target, source
241:                else:
242:                    # Tie on ship count: smaller id wins (deterministic).
243:                    if str(source.id) < str(target.id):
244:                        winner, loser = source, target
245:                    else:
246:                        winner, loser = target, source
247:                logger.warning(
248:                    f"[BUG-122] Mutual JOIN_FLEET: Fleet {loser.id} ({len(loser.ships)} ships) "
249:                    f"absorbed by Fleet {winner.id} ({len(winner.ships)} ships)"
250:                )
251:                result.append((empire, loser, winner))
252:                consumed.add(source)
253:                consumed.add(target)
254:            else:
255:                result.append((empire, source, target))
256:                consumed.add(source)
257:
258:        return result
259:
260:    def _emit_join_cancelled(
261:        self,
262:        fleet: Fleet,
263:        target_fleet: Fleet,
264:        empire: "Empire",
265:        *,
266:        reason: str,
267:    ) -> None:
268:        """Emit a FLEET_JOIN_CANCELLED event with a structured reason field.
269:
270:        BUG-122: reasons used by process_instant_orders are
271:        `absorbed_by_other_merge` and `target_absorbed_mid_iteration`.
272:        Fleet.merge_with uses `self_target_after_redirect`. Empire.remove_fleet
273:        uses an unstructured cancellation message (PROJ-222 path).
274:        """
275:        self._emit_event(
276:            EventType.FLEET_JOIN_CANCELLED,
277:            category=EventCategory.FLEET_OPERATIONS,
278:            empire_id=empire.id,
279:            message=f"Fleet {fleet.id} join order cancelled: {reason}",
280:            fleet_id=fleet.id,
281:            target_fleet_id=target_fleet.id,
282:            reason=reason,
283:        )

17:def _wire_catalog_on(engine, empire_id, data):
18:    """PROJ-427 Phase 3 helper: wire a per-empire catalog map onto the
19:    engine's spawner with ``data`` available under all test design_ids.
20:    """
21:    from game.strategy.systems.design_catalog import DesignCatalog
22:    cat = DesignCatalog(empire_id=empire_id)
23:    for did in (
24:        "scout_design", "cruiser_design", "bad_design", "scout",
25:        "fighter_design", "complex_design", "complex_xyz", "shipyard",
26:    ):
27:        cat._data_by_id[did] = data
28:    engine._spawner.set_design_catalogs_by_empire({empire_id: cat})
29:    return cat

36:def _capture_log_event_calls():
37:    """Return a (calls_list, fake_handler, EventBus) tuple.
38:
39:    The EventBus routes events through the fake handler so engines that use
40:    ``self._event_bus.log_event(...)`` will populate the calls list.
41:    """
42:    calls = []
43:
44:    def _fake_log_event(event_type, **kwargs):
45:        calls.append((event_type, kwargs))
46:
47:    bus = EventBus(_fake_log_event)
48:    return calls, _fake_log_event, bus

434:    def _make_colonize_fleet(self):
435:        """Create a fleet with a COLONIZE order."""
436:        from game.strategy.data.fleet import Fleet
437:        from game.strategy.data.order_types import Order, OrderType
438:        from enum import Enum
439:
440:        class MockPlanetType(Enum):
441:            CONTINENTAL = "CONTINENTAL"
442:
443:        fleet = MagicMock(spec=Fleet)
444:        fleet.id = 3
445:        fleet.owner_id = 0
446:        fleet.location = MagicMock(q=2, r=-1)
447:
448:        # Mock ship with drop pod in carried_items
449:        mock_ship = MagicMock()
450:        mock_ship.name = "Colony Ship"
451:        mock_ship.bay_inventory = BayInventory(bay=[], pods=[DropPod(
452:            design_id="test_pod", design_data={"layers": {"CORE": []}},
453:            mass=500.0, payload={"name": "Test Pod", "vehicle_type": "drop_pod"},
454:        )])
455:        fleet.ships = [mock_ship]
456:
457:        target_planet = _make_mock_planet(planet_id=10, name="New Earth")
458:        target_planet.owner_id = None
459:        target_planet.populations = []
460:        target_planet.planet_type = MockPlanetType.CONTINENTAL
461:
462:        order = Order(OrderType.COLONIZE, target=target_planet)
463:        fleet.get_current_order.return_value = order
464:        fleet.pop_order = MagicMock()
465:
466:        return fleet, target_planet

468:    def _make_component_registry(self):
469:        """Create component registry for tests."""
470:        return {
471:            'colony_pod': {
472:                'id': 'colony_pod',
473:                'abilities': {'ColonizePlanet': True}
474:            },
475:        }

477:    def test_process_colonize_emits_colony_founded_event(self):
478:        """process_colonize() emits colony_founded on success."""
479:        from game.strategy.engine.order_processor import OrderProcessor
480:
481:        calls, fake, bus = _capture_log_event_calls()
482:        processor = OrderProcessor(event_bus=bus)
483:        fleet, target_planet = self._make_colonize_fleet()
484:        empire = _make_mock_empire(name="Human Empire")
485:        galaxy = _make_mock_galaxy()
486:        component_registry = self._make_component_registry()
487:
488:        with patch('game.strategy.validation.ColonizeValidator') as mock_val:
489:            mock_result = MagicMock()
490:            mock_result.is_valid = True
491:            mock_val.validate.return_value = mock_result
492:            mock_val.fleet_has_drop_pod.return_value = True
493:            mock_val.find_ship_with_drop_pod.return_value = (fleet.ships[0], 0)
494:
495:            with patch.object(processor._handler_registry.get(OrderType.COLONIZE), '_deploy_drop_pod'):
496:                result = processor.get_handler(OrderType.COLONIZE).execute_action_order(
497:                    fleet, empire, galaxy, component_registry=component_registry)
498:
499:        assert result.colonized is True
500:        assert len(calls) == 1
501:        etype, kw = calls[0]
502:        assert etype == EventType.COLONY_FOUNDED
503:        assert kw["category"] == EventCategory.COLONIES
504:        assert kw["empire_id"] == 0
505:        assert "New Earth" in kw["message"]
506:        assert kw["planet_id"] == 10
507:        assert kw["planet_name"] == "New Earth"

606:    def test_simulated_combat_emits_combat_resolved_event(self):
607:        """`_resolve_combat_at_hex` emits one combat_resolved event per
608:        battle (BUG-126: schema migrated to `participating_fleet_ids` /
609:        `surviving_fleet_ids` / `destroyed_fleet_ids`)."""
610:        from game.strategy.engine.conflict_resolution_engine import ConflictResolutionEngine
611:        from game.strategy.data.fleet import Fleet
612:        from game.core.hex_math import HexCoord
613:
614:        mock_resolver = MagicMock()
615:        mock_result = MagicMock()
616:        mock_result.winner = 0
617:        mock_result.team_survivors = {0: [MagicMock()], 1: []}
618:
619:        # Simulate the PostBattleHook wiping team 1's fleet.
620:        def _resolve_battle(fleets, **_kw):
621:            list(fleets)[1].ships = []
622:            return mock_result
623:
624:        mock_resolver.resolve_battle.side_effect = _resolve_battle
625:
626:        calls, fake, bus = _capture_log_event_calls()
627:        engine = ConflictResolutionEngine(battle_resolver=mock_resolver, event_bus=bus)
628:        engine._empires = []
629:        engine._combats_resolved = 0
630:        engine._fleets_destroyed = []
631:
632:        emp1 = MagicMock(); emp1.id = 0; emp1.remove_fleet = MagicMock()
633:        emp2 = MagicMock(); emp2.id = 1; emp2.remove_fleet = MagicMock()
634:        f1 = MagicMock(spec=Fleet); f1.id = 1; f1.owner_id = 0
635:        f1.location = HexCoord(3, 4); f1.ships = [MagicMock()]
636:        f2 = MagicMock(spec=Fleet); f2.id = 2; f2.owner_id = 1
637:        f2.location = HexCoord(3, 4); f2.ships = [MagicMock()]
638:
639:        engine._resolve_combat_at_hex([(emp1, f1), (emp2, f2)])
640:
641:        assert len(calls) == 1
642:        etype, kw = calls[0]
643:        assert etype == EventType.COMBAT_RESOLVED
644:        assert kw["category"] == EventCategory.COMBAT
645:        assert sorted(kw["participating_fleet_ids"]) == [1, 2]
646:        assert kw["surviving_fleet_ids"] == [1]
647:        assert kw["destroyed_fleet_ids"] == [2]
648:

104:def make_colony_ship(name: str, owner_id: int, pod_type: str, registries=None) -> ShipInstance:
105:    """Create a ship with a drop pod in carried_items.
106:
107:    Phase 3: Drop pods are carried items. Ship is reusable after colonization.
108:    """
109:    ship = ShipInstance(
110:        instance_id=f"colony-{name.lower().replace(' ', '-')}-{id(name)}",
111:        design_id=f"{pod_type}_colony_ship",
112:        name=name,
113:        owner_id=owner_id,
114:        design_data={
115:            'name': name,
116:            'vehicle_type': 'Ship',
117:            'stats': {'mass': 100},
118:            'expected_stats': {'speed': 10.0},
119:            'layers': {
120:                'HULL': [{'id': 'colony_pod_bay'}]
121:            }
122:        },
123:    )
124:    # PROJ-436 Phase 9: typed DropPod into bay_inventory.pods.
125:    from game.strategy.data.bay_inventory import DropPod
126:    ship.bay_inventory.pods.append(DropPod(
127:        design_id=f"{pod_type.lower()}_drop_pod",
128:        design_data={"layers": {"CORE": []}},
129:        mass=500.0,
130:        payload={
131:            "name": f"Drop Pod ({pod_type})",
132:            "vehicle_type": "drop_pod",
133:        },
134:    ))
135:    if registries is not None:
136:        ship.set_registries(registries)
137:    return ship

254:class TestColonizeWithMatchingPod:
255:    """Tests for colonization with matching colony pod."""
256:
257:    def test_colonize_with_matching_pod_succeeds(
258:        self, galaxy_with_ice_planet, component_registry, fresh_registries
259:    ):
260:        """
261:        PROJ-55: Colonization succeeds when fleet has matching pod.
262:        PROJ-211: Updated to pass registries for DI compliance.
263:
264:        Create Ice Dwarf planet, fleet with Ice Dwarf pod ship.
265:        Issue colonize command.
266:        Execute colonization.
267:        Assert: Planet colonized, colony ship removed, fleet remains.
268:        """
269:        galaxy, ice_planet = galaxy_with_ice_planet
270:
271:        # Create fleet with ice dwarf colony ship and combat ship
272:        # PROJ-211: Pass registries for DI compliance
273:        colony_ship = make_colony_ship("Ice Colony Ship", 1, "ICE_DWARF", fresh_registries)
274:        combat_ship = make_combat_ship("Escort Ship", 1, fresh_registries)
275:
276:        fleet = Fleet(1, 1, HexCoord(10, 10))
277:        fleet.ships.append(colony_ship)
278:        fleet.ships.append(combat_ship)
279:        fleet.orders.append(Order(OrderType.COLONIZE, ice_planet))
280:
281:        empire = Empire(1, "Player 1", (255, 0, 0))
282:        empire.fleets.append(fleet)
283:
284:        # Execute colonization with component registry
285:        processor = OrderProcessor()
286:        result = processor.get_handler(OrderType.COLONIZE).execute_action_order(
287:            fleet, empire, galaxy, component_registry=component_registry)
288:
289:        # Assert: Colonization succeeded
290:        assert result.colonized is True
291:        assert ice_planet.owner_id == 1
292:        assert ice_planet in empire.colonies
293:
294:        # Phase 3: Both ships stay (ship is reusable)
295:        assert colony_ship in fleet.ships
296:        assert combat_ship in fleet.ships
297:        assert len(fleet.ships) == 2
298:
299:        # PROJ-436 Phase 9: drop pod consumed from typed bay_inventory.pods.
300:        assert len(colony_ship.bay_inventory.pods) == 0
301:
302:        # Fleet still exists
303:        assert fleet in empire.fleets

349:    ):
350:        """
351:        PROJ-55: Chain colonization works with multiple pods.
352:        PROJ-211: Updated to pass registries for DI compliance.
353:
354:        Create 2 Continental planets, fleet with 2 Continental pod ships.
355:        Queue both colonizations.
356:        Execute turn.
357:        Assert: Both planets colonized, both ships removed.
358:        """
359:        galaxy, continental1, continental2 = galaxy_with_two_continental_planets

362:        # PROJ-211: Pass registries for DI compliance
363:        colony_ship1 = make_colony_ship("Colony Ship 1", 1, "CONTINENTAL", fresh_registries)
364:        colony_ship2 = make_colony_ship("Colony Ship 2", 1, "CONTINENTAL", fresh_registries)
365:

367:        fleet.ships.append(colony_ship1)
368:        fleet.ships.append(colony_ship2)
369:        fleet.orders.append(Order(OrderType.COLONIZE, continental1))
370:        fleet.orders.append(Order(OrderType.COLONIZE, continental2))

373:        empire.fleets.append(fleet)

379:            fleet, empire, galaxy, component_registry=component_registry)

387:            fleet, empire, galaxy, component_registry=component_registry)

389:        assert continental2.owner_id == 1

594:    def test_remaining_pods_after_commitment(
595:        self, galaxy_with_three_ice_planets, component_registry
596:    ):
597:        """
598:        Phase 3: Remaining drop pods correctly calculated after orders queued.
599:
600:        Fleet with 2 drop pods, queue 1 colonization.
601:        Assert: Remaining = 1 drop pod.
602:        """
603:        galaxy, ice1, ice2, ice3 = galaxy_with_three_ice_planets
604:
605:        # Create fleet with 2 colony ships (2 drop pods)
606:        colony_ship1 = make_colony_ship("Ice Colony 1", 1, "ICE_DWARF")
607:        colony_ship2 = make_colony_ship("Ice Colony 2", 1, "ICE_DWARF")
608:
609:        fleet = Fleet(1, 1, HexCoord(10, 10))
610:        fleet.ships.append(colony_ship1)
611:        fleet.ships.append(colony_ship2)
612:
613:        # Before any orders
614:        available = ColonizeValidator.count_drop_pods(fleet)
615:        committed = ColonizeValidator.count_committed_colonize_orders(fleet)
616:        assert available == 2
617:        assert committed == 0
618:
619:        # Queue 1 colonization
620:        fleet.orders.append(Order(OrderType.COLONIZE, ice1))
621:
622:        # After 1 order
623:        committed_after = ColonizeValidator.count_committed_colonize_orders(fleet)
624:        assert committed_after == 1
625:
626:        # Remaining = available - committed = 2 - 1 = 1
627:        remaining = available - committed_after
628:        assert remaining == 1
