24:
25:class InstantBattleResolver(IBattleResolver):
26:    """Battle resolver that instantly declares team 0 the winner.
27:
28:    BUG-126: simulates the `PostBattleHook` by wiping the loser fleets'
29:    ship lists AND removing them from their owning empire. Production
30:    callers receive this side-effect via `apply_outcome_to_fleets` +
31:    `_prune_empty_fleets`; here we hand-roll it because the hook is
32:    bypassed when callers inject a mock resolver."""
33:
34:    def __init__(self, empires_by_team=None):
35:        self._empires_by_team = empires_by_team or {}
36:
37:    def resolve_battle(self, fleets, modifiers=None, seed=None, registries=None,
38:                       environmental_effects=None, empires=None):
39:        fleet_list = list(fleets)
40:        survivors = {i: [] for i in range(len(fleet_list))}
41:        survivors[0] = list(
42:            fleet_list[0].battle.to_battle_ships(team_id=0, registries=registries)
43:        )
44:        # Simulate the PostBattleHook: wipe loser ship lists and prune
45:        # empty fleets from their empires.
46:        empires_by_team = empires or self._empires_by_team
47:        for tid, fleet in enumerate(fleet_list[1:], start=1):
48:            fleet.ships = []
49:            empire = empires_by_team.get(tid)
50:            if empire is not None and fleet in empire.fleets:
51:                empire.remove_fleet(fleet)
52:        return BattleResult(
53:            winner=0,
54:            tick_count=1,
55:            team_survivors=survivors,
56:        )
57:
58:

70:    gal = make_galaxy_stub(radius=300)
71:    gal.warp_points = []
72:    return gal

102:def _make_fleet(fleet_id, owner_id, location, ship_count=1, registries=None):
103:    """Create a fleet with mock ships."""
104:    fleet = Fleet(fleet_id, owner_id, location, speed=15.0)
105:    for i in range(ship_count):
106:        ship = make_mock_ship_instance(
107:            f"Ship-{fleet_id}-{i}", owner_id, registries=registries
108:        )
109:        fleet.ships.append(ship)
110:    return fleet

112:
113:def _assert_registered(galaxy, fleet):
114:    """Assert fleet IS in the galaxy registry."""
115:    found = galaxy.get_fleet_by_id(fleet.id)
116:    assert found is fleet, (
117:        f"Fleet {fleet.id} should be in galaxy registry but was not found"
118:    )
119:
120:
121:def _assert_not_registered(galaxy, fleet_id):
122:    """Assert fleet is NOT in the galaxy registry."""
123:    found = galaxy.get_fleet_by_id(fleet_id)
124:    assert found is None, (
125:        f"Fleet {fleet_id} should NOT be in galaxy registry but was found"
126:    )

130:# Task 4.2: Combat destruction unregisters fleet
131:# ===========================================================================
132:
133:class TestCombatUnregistersDestroyedFleet:
134:    """Verify conflict_resolution_engine.py:186 auto-unregisters loser fleet."""
135:
136:    def test_combat_unregisters_destroyed_fleet(
137:        self, galaxy, two_empires, fresh_registries
138:    ):
139:        """When a fleet is destroyed in combat, it is removed from galaxy registry."""
140:        emp1, emp2 = two_empires
141:        loc = HexCoord(10, 10)
142:
143:        # Create fleets at same location (conflict)
144:        fleet1 = _make_fleet(100, emp1.id, loc, ship_count=1, registries=fresh_registries)
145:        fleet2 = _make_fleet(200, emp2.id, loc, ship_count=1, registries=fresh_registries)
146:        emp1.add_fleet(fleet1)
147:        emp2.add_fleet(fleet2)
148:
149:        # Both should be registered
150:        _assert_registered(galaxy, fleet1)
151:        _assert_registered(galaxy, fleet2)
152:
153:        # Resolve combat using instant resolver to skip full simulation.
154:        # BUG-126: the resolver also simulates the PostBattleHook
155:        # (wiping loser ships + pruning the fleet from its empire),
156:        # because the strategy engine no longer prunes fleets itself.
157:        engine = ConflictResolutionEngine(
158:            battle_resolver=InstantBattleResolver(empires_by_team={0: emp1, 1: emp2}),
159:            registries=fresh_registries,
160:        )
161:        # PROJ-320: speed-15 fleets have opportunity every 6 ticks.
162:        result = engine.resolve_all_conflicts(
163:            [emp1, emp2], galaxy, tick=6, moved_fleet_ids=set(),
164:        )
165:
166:        # Exactly one fleet should have been destroyed
167:        assert result.combats_resolved == 1
168:        assert len(result.fleets_destroyed) == 1
169:
170:        destroyed_id = result.fleets_destroyed[0]
171:
172:        # Destroyed fleet must NOT be in galaxy registry
173:        _assert_not_registered(galaxy, destroyed_id)
174:
175:        # Surviving fleet must still be in galaxy registry
176:        survivor_id = 100 if destroyed_id == 200 else 200
177:        survivor_fleet = galaxy.get_fleet_by_id(survivor_id)
178:        assert survivor_fleet is not None, (
179:            f"Surviving fleet {survivor_id} should still be in galaxy registry"
180:        )

396:        result = processor._finalize_superweapon(
397:            fleet=fleet,
398:            empire=emp1,
399:            ship=ship,
400:            event_type=EventType.SHIPS_SELF_DESTRUCTED,
401:            event_message="Test superweapon",
402:            log_message="Test superweapon finalize",
403:        )
404:
405:        assert result.success is True
406:        assert result.fleet_consumed is True
407:        _assert_not_registered(galaxy, 602)
408:
409:
410:# ===========================================================================
411:# Task 4.6: Save/load preserves fleet registry
412:# ===========================================================================
413:
414:class TestSaveLoadPreservesFleetRegistry:
415:    """Verify GameSession.from_dict() restores fleet registry correctly."""
416:
417:    def test_save_load_preserves_fleet_registry(
418:        self, small_game_session, fresh_registries
419:    ):
420:        """All fleets are in galaxy registry after save/load cycle."""
421:        session = small_game_session
422:
423:        # Serialize and deserialize
424:        data = session.to_dict()
425:        restored = GameSession.from_dict(data)
426:
427:        # All fleets should be registered in the restored galaxy
428:        for empire in restored.empires:
429:            for fleet in empire.fleets:
430:                found = restored.galaxy.get_fleet_by_id(fleet.id)
431:                assert found is fleet, (
432:                    f"Fleet {fleet.id} should be in registry after load"
433:                )
434:
435:    def test_new_fleet_after_load_auto_registers(
436:        self, small_game_session, fresh_registries
437:    ):
438:        """Fleets added after save/load cycle are automatically registered."""
439:        session = small_game_session
440:
441:        data = session.to_dict()
442:        restored = GameSession.from_dict(data)
443:
444:        empire = restored.empires[0]
445:        new_fleet = Fleet(7777, empire.id, HexCoord(5, 5), speed=15.0)
446:        new_fleet.ships = [
447:            make_mock_ship_instance("Scout", empire.id, registries=fresh_registries)
448:        ]
449:        empire.add_fleet(new_fleet)
450:
451:        # New fleet should be auto-registered
452:        found = restored.galaxy.get_fleet_by_id(7777)
453:        assert found is new_fleet, (
454:            "Fleet added after load should be auto-registered with galaxy"
455:        )
456:
457:    def test_remove_fleet_after_load_unregisters(
458:        self, small_game_session, fresh_registries
459:    ):
460:        """Fleets removed after save/load are automatically unregistered."""
461:        session = small_game_session
462:
463:        # A fresh GameSession starts empires with colonies but no fleets,
464:        # so we explicitly seed one before the save/load cycle to exercise
465:        # the post-restore removal path. Constructed inline rather than
466:        # via a shared save fixture to keep this test self-contained.
467:        pre_save_empire = session.empires[0]
468:        seeded_fleet = Fleet(8888, pre_save_empire.id, HexCoord(3, 3), speed=15.0)
469:        seeded_fleet.ships = [
470:            make_mock_ship_instance(
471:                "Scout", pre_save_empire.id, registries=fresh_registries
472:            )
473:        ]
474:        pre_save_empire.add_fleet(seeded_fleet)
475:
476:        data = session.to_dict()
477:        restored = GameSession.from_dict(data)
478:
479:        # Locate the same fleet in the restored session.
480:        restored_empire = next(
481:            emp for emp in restored.empires if emp.id == pre_save_empire.id
482:        )
483:        target_fleet = next(f for f in restored_empire.fleets if f.id == 8888)
484:
485:        fleet_id = target_fleet.id
486:        _assert_registered(restored.galaxy, target_fleet)
487:
488:        # Remove fleet
489:        restored_empire.remove_fleet(target_fleet)
490:
491:        # Should be unregistered
492:        _assert_not_registered(restored.galaxy, fleet_id)
493:

Representative migrated call sites use the handler-direct path in `tests/integration/colonization/test_explicit_orders.py:63-66,89-93`, `tests/integration/colonization/test_planet_specific_colonization.py:285-287`, and `tests/integration/strategy/test_fleet_registration_lifecycle.py:211-214`. |
+| F-B-018 | closed | `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/base.py:37-61` now documents `OrderExecutionResult` as the live unified surface and no longer labels the five per-handler fields as “legacy”. The retained fields all have live producers/readers: `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/join_fleet.py:73-78`, `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/colonize.py:138-143`, `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/transfer.py:215-216`; readers include `tests/integration/strategy/test_fleet_registration_lifecycle.py:214`, `tests/integration/colonization/test_planet_specific_colonization.py:290`, and `tests/integration/colonization/test_explicit_orders.py:93`. |
+
+- I spot-checked the Phase 3 colonize-call migration for the reported one-shot-script bug and did not find malformed duplicated kwargs; representative calls are syntactically correct in `tests/integration/colonization/test_planet_specific_colonization.py:286-287`, `tests/unit/strategy/engine/test_process_colonize_validation.py:201-202`, and `tests/unit/strategy/test_engine_event_emission.py:984-985`.
+- The tightened `OrderType` cap looks correct against the current facade: the only runtime `OrderType.*` AST reference left in `game/strategy/engine/order_processor.py` is `OrderType.JOIN_FLEET` at `game/strategy/engine/order_processor.py:88`, and the guard at `tests/unit/strategy/engine/order_handlers/test_order_processor_facade.py:32-57` allows `<= 2`, which still catches a reintroduced branch ladder.
+- I do not think a replacement re-emergence guard for the deleted `test_component_inspector_surface.py` is warranted. `component_inspector` was an internal shim, there are no live imports or `patch(...)` targets left, and the canonical surfaces are directly tested in `tests/unit/strategy/services/test_component_abilities.py:16-26` and `tests/unit/strategy/services/test_component_layers.py:12`.
+
+## Side-effects / regressions
+
+- `game/strategy/services/system_effects_collector.py:83-86` still says effect metadata lives in `effect_ability_metadata.EFFECT_ABILITY_METADATA`. That comment is now wrong; the live source of truth is `ability_metadata.py` via `get_ability_metadata(...).effect`.
+- `game/strategy/services/ability_metadata.py:111-114` still describes the deleted shim in present tense (“The shim module ... derives ...”). That should be refreshed or past-tensed to avoid implying the shim still exists.
+- Several touched Phase 3 tests still narrate the deleted facade methods even though they now dispatch through handlers directly: `tests/unit/strategy/engine/test_process_colonize_validation.py:2-6,176,312`, `tests/unit/strategy/engine/test_fleet_order_transfer.py:91`, `tests/unit/strategy/test_engine_event_emission.py:477-478`, and `tests/integration/strategy/test_fleet_registration_lifecycle.py:210`.
+
+## Out-of-scope observations
+
+- The handler-module docstrings that say “Lifted from `OrderProcessor.process_*`” read as provenance, not as live API promises, so I would not treat them as regressions by themselves: `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/colonize.py:1-17`, `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/transfer.py:1-31`, `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/join_fleet.py:1-22`.
+- I did not find an additional behavior bug in the touched files that rises to `discovered_issues/log.jsonl` severity. The remaining residue I saw is documentation/test narration cleanup, not a new runtime defect.
+
+## Summary
+
+- Overall: all four findings are closed in live code on `group-b` / `456d12b36`; the remaining issues are low-severity comment/docstring/test-narration residue, not surviving shim/facade behavior.
+
+## Risks
+
+- I did not run tests because the request explicitly set `allow_tests: false`. This audit is based on current-source inspection, grep, and the `group-b` compare range only.
+- A few project checklist claims about “zero grep” closure are only true if you ignore historical comments/docstrings. Future audits that use raw grep without that nuance will get false positives.
+

+# PROJ-454 audit response
+
+## Findings
+
+## Verdict table
+| Finding | Status | Evidence |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| F-B-004 | closed | `git diff --name-status 82b751fe0..456d12b36` deletes `game/strategy/services/effect_ability_metadata.py`; callers now use the canonical facet path in `game/strategy/services/effect_ability_display.py:20-34` and `game/strategy/services/system_effects_collector.py:42-49,243-245`; the migrated regression coverage reads `get_ability_metadata(...).effect` in `tests/unit/strategy/services/test_ability_metadata_effects.py:11-19,53-68`. |
+| F-B-005 | closed | `git diff --name-status 82b751fe0..456d12b36` deletes `game/strategy/services/component_inspector.py`; `git grep -n "game.strategy.services.component_inspector\\." -- tests` and `git grep -n "from game.strategy.services.component_inspector import" -- game tests` both return zero live callers; canonical imports are visible in `game/strategy/data/ship_instance.py:636,656,666`, `tests/unit/strategy/services/test_component_abilities.py:16-26`, and `tests/unit/strategy/services/test_component_layers.py:12`. |
+| F-B-017 | closed | `game/strategy/engine/order_processor.py:61-115` now exposes only `get_handler(...)`, `process_instant_orders(...)`, and `execute_action_order(...)`; the legacy `process_join_fleet` / `process_colonize` / `process_transfer` helpers and their typed result dataclasses are gone. Representative migrated call sites use the handler-direct path in `tests/integration/colonization/test_explicit_orders.py:63-66,89-93`, `tests/integration/colonization/test_planet_specific_colonization.py:285-287`, and `tests/integration/strategy/test_fleet_registration_lifecycle.py:211-214`. |
+| F-B-018 | closed | `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/base.py:37-61` now documents `OrderExecutionResult` as the live unified surface and no longer labels the five per-handler fields as “legacy”. The retained fields all have live producers/readers: `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/join_fleet.py:73-78`, `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/colonize.py:138-143`, `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/transfer.py:215-216`; readers include `tests/integration/strategy/test_fleet_registration_lifecycle.py:214`, `tests/integration/colonization/test_planet_specific_colonization.py:290`, and `tests/integration/colonization/test_explicit_orders.py:93`. |
+
+- I spot-checked the Phase 3 colonize-call migration for the reported one-shot-script bug and did not find malformed duplicated kwargs; representative calls are syntactically correct in `tests/integration/colonization/test_planet_specific_colonization.py:286-287`, `tests/unit/strategy/engine/test_process_colonize_validation.py:201-202`, and `tests/unit/strategy/test_engine_event_emission.py:984-985`.
+- The tightened `OrderType` cap looks correct against the current facade: the only runtime `OrderType.*` AST reference left in `game/strategy/engine/order_processor.py` is `OrderType.JOIN_FLEET` at `game/strategy/engine/order_processor.py:88`, and the guard at `tests/unit/strategy/engine/order_handlers/test_order_processor_facade.py:32-57` allows `<= 2`, which still catches a reintroduced branch ladder.
+- I do not think a replacement re-emergence guard for the deleted `test_component_inspector_surface.py` is warranted. `component_inspector` was an internal shim, there are no live imports or `patch(...)` targets left, and the canonical surfaces are directly tested in `tests/unit/strategy/services/test_component_abilities.py:16-26` and `tests/unit/strategy/services/test_component_layers.py:12`.
+
+## Side-effects / regressions
+
+- `game/strategy/services/system_effects_collector.py:83-86` still says effect metadata lives in `effect_ability_metadata.EFFECT_ABILITY_METADATA`. That comment is now wrong; the live source of truth is `ability_metadata.py` via `get_ability_metadata(...).effect`.
+- `game/strategy/services/ability_metadata.py:111-114` still describes the deleted shim in present tense (“The shim module ... derives ...”). That should be refreshed or past-tensed to avoid implying the shim still exists.
+- Several touched Phase 3 tests still narrate the deleted facade methods even though they now dispatch through handlers directly: `tests/unit/strategy/engine/test_process_colonize_validation.py:2-6,176,312`, `tests/unit/strategy/engine/test_fleet_order_transfer.py:91`, `tests/unit/strategy/test_engine_event_emission.py:477-478`, and `tests/integration/strategy/test_fleet_registration_lifecycle.py:210`.
+
+## Out-of-scope observations
+
+- The handler-module docstrings that say “Lifted from `OrderProcessor.process_*`” read as provenance, not as live API promises, so I would not treat them as regressions by themselves: `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/colonize.py:1-17`, `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/transfer.py:1-31`, `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/join_fleet.py:1-22`.
+- I did not find an additional behavior bug in the touched files that rises to `discovered_issues/log.jsonl` severity. The remaining residue I saw is documentation/test narration cleanup, not a new runtime defect.
+
+## Summary
+
+- Overall: all four findings are closed in live code on `group-b` / `456d12b36`; the remaining issues are low-severity comment/docstring/test-narration residue, not surviving shim/facade behavior.
+
+## Risks
+
+- I did not run tests because the request explicitly set `allow_tests: false`. This audit is based on current-source inspection, grep, and the `group-b` compare range only.
+- A few project checklist claims about “zero grep” closure are only true if you ignore historical comments/docstrings. Future audits that use raw grep without that nuance will get false positives.
+
+## Open questions
+
+None.

- Several touched Phase 3 tests still narrate the deleted facade methods even though they now dispatch through handlers directly: `tests/unit/strategy/engine/test_process_colonize_validation.py:2-6,176,312`, `tests/unit/strategy/engine/test_fleet_order_transfer.py:91`, `tests/unit/strategy/test_engine_event_emission.py:477-478`, and `tests/integration/strategy/test_fleet_registration_lifecycle.py:210`.

- I did not find an additional behavior bug in the touched files that rises to `discovered_issues/log.jsonl` severity. The remaining residue I saw is documentation/test narration cleanup, not a new runtime defect.

+
+- I spot-checked the Phase 3 colonize-call migration for the reported one-shot-script bug and did not find malformed duplicated kwargs; representative calls are syntactically correct in `tests/integration/colonization/test_planet_specific_colonization.py:286-287`, `tests/unit/strategy/engine/test_process_colonize_validation.py:201-202`, and `tests/unit/strategy/test_engine_event_emission.py:984-985`.
+- The tightened `OrderType` cap looks correct against the current facade: the only runtime `OrderType.*` AST reference left in `game/strategy/engine/order_processor.py` is `OrderType.JOIN_FLEET` at `game/strategy/engine/order_processor.py:88`, and the guard at `tests/unit/strategy/engine/order_handlers/test_order_processor_facade.py:32-57` allows `<= 2`, which still catches a reintroduced branch ladder.
+- I do not think a replacement re-emergence guard for the deleted `test_component_inspector_surface.py` is warranted. `component_inspector` was an internal shim, there are no live imports or `patch(...)` targets left, and the canonical surfaces are directly tested in `tests/unit/strategy/services/test_component_abilities.py:16-26` and `tests/unit/strategy/services/test_component_layers.py:12`.
+
+## Side-effects / regressions
+
+- `game/strategy/services/system_effects_collector.py:83-86` still says effect metadata lives in `effect_ability_metadata.EFFECT_ABILITY_METADATA`. That comment is now wrong; the live source of truth is `ability_metadata.py` via `get_ability_metadata(...).effect`.
+- `game/strategy/services/ability_metadata.py:111-114` still describes the deleted shim in present tense (“The shim module ... derives ...”). That should be refreshed or past-tensed to avoid implying the shim still exists.
+- Several touched Phase 3 tests still narrate the deleted facade methods even though they now dispatch through handlers directly: `tests/unit/strategy/engine/test_process_colonize_validation.py:2-6,176,312`, `tests/unit/strategy/engine/test_fleet_order_transfer.py:91`, `tests/unit/strategy/test_engine_event_emission.py:477-478`, and `tests/integration/strategy/test_fleet_registration_lifecycle.py:210`.
+
+## Out-of-scope observations
+
+- The handler-module docstrings that say “Lifted from `OrderProcessor.process_*`” read as provenance, not as live API promises, so I would not treat them as regressions by themselves: `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/colonize.py:1-17`, `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/transfer.py:1-31`, `game/strategy/engine/order_handlers/join_fleet.py:1-22`.
+- I did not find an additional behavior bug in the touched files that rises to `discovered_issues/log.jsonl` severity. The remaining residue I saw is documentation/test narration cleanup, not a new runtime defect.
+
+## Summary
+
+- Overall: all four findings are closed in live code on `group-b` / `456d12b36`; the remaining issues are low-severity comment/docstring/test-narration residue, not surviving shim/facade behavior.
+
+## Risks
+
+- I did not run tests because the request explicitly set `allow_tests: false`. This audit is based on current-source inspection, grep, and the `group-b` compare range only.
+- A few project checklist claims about “zero grep” closure are only true if you ignore historical comments/docstrings. Future audits that use raw grep without that nuance will get false positives.
+
+## Open questions
+
+None.

+## Side-effects / regressions
+
+- `game/strategy/services/system_effects_collector.py:83-86` still says effect metadata lives in `effect_ability_metadata.EFFECT_ABILITY_METADATA`. That comment is now wrong; the live source of truth is `ability_metadata.py` via `get_ability_metadata(...).effect`.
+- `game/strategy/services/ability_metadata.py:111-114` still describes the deleted shim in present tense ("The shim module ... derives ..."). That should be refreshed or past-tensed to avoid implying the shim still exists.
+- Several touched Phase 3 tests still narrate the deleted facade methods even though they now dispatch through handlers directly: `tests/unit/strategy/engine/test_process_colonize_validation.py:2-6,176,312`, `tests/unit/strategy/engine/test_fleet_order_transfer.py:91`, `tests/unit/strategy/test_engine_event_emission.py:477-478`, and `tests/integration/strategy/test_fleet_registration_lifecycle.py:210`.
+
+## Out-of-scope observations
+
